# Symbol renames of 2026-09-28: "OLD NEW" per line; apply with tools/apply_renames.py
# (re-runnable: lines already applied are skipped).  Sections, in priority order:
# names ported from FireEmblem7J, then from fireemblem8u (functions matched by
# code, calls, table slots or position between matched neighbours; data by the
# literals of matched functions or the slots of same-named tables); then names
# derived from what the data is:
#   graphics   the entry's data/graphics.txt NAME: Img_/Pal_/Tsa_ (4bpp, palette,
#              tsa/tilemap) + the path: portrait/NNN_Who_face -> Img_Portrait_Who_Face
#              (the id is kept when a name has two portraits or none), bg_XX ->
#              Img_Bg_XX, btl_bg -> BattleBg, cg -> Cg, map layout/palette/tile
#              config/object set -> MapLayout_ChXX / Pal_Map_ChXX / TileConfig_XX /
#              Img_MapObj_XX, unit_icon/move -> Img_Mu_<Class>, banim/NNN_abbr ->
#              Img_Banim_NNN_abbr_SheetN / BanimOam_NNN_abbr_L|R / Pal_Banim_NNN_abbr,
#              TsaArray_X_NN / ImgLut_X_NN elements -> Tsa_X_NN / Img_X_NN;
#   structure  the table that holds it (FaceInfoTable, gBackgroundTable, TextGlyphs_*,
#              banim_data) or the AnimScr_X that uses it (AnimSprite_X_ADDR);
#   procs      the named proc script that runs a function (ProcScr_X: X_Loop, X_Init,
#              X_OnEnd) or that it starts (StartX; StartSubSpell_X and
#              StartSpellAnim<Spell> in the spell effects, fireemblem8u's convention).
